# Replay / Production Business-Logic Parity

**Revision:** RPR-0.6-DRAFT  
**Status:** NOT APPROVED Г?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
**Baseline:** `0cb6a9a6cc64ba8ac9d33797bcc30af534fd46e3`; the complete dirty-worktree snapshot must be re-hashed and approved immediately before implementation.  
**Supersedes:** RPR-0.4/RPR-0.5 for review only.

## 1. Business objective and flow

Replay must use current Production paper-trading policies with an isolated
virtual account. It must never read Production account state, call HTTP, or
call candle-only output historical parity.

1. User selects one symbol, virtual profile, window and mode.
2. Qualifier validates every input before a generated Replay artifact exists.
3. A refusal writes only a run/certificate diagnostic.
4. Qualified analysis uses closed, identity-matching candles.
5. Simulation invokes pure Production policies with only virtual adapters.
6. Results stage privately and publish only on terminal success.

Release 1 labels outcomes only `ANALYSIS_PARITY`,
`PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`, or `REFUSED_INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`.
`ENTRY_DECISION_PARITY` and historical wallet-lifecycle parity are out of scope:
they require account-state evidence that Replay is forbidden to read.

## 2. Root cause, evidence and scope

Replay currently can include open candles, has null IDs, reads
`wallet_settings`, and differs from Production price/signal management.
Evidence is current source in `RegressionTestWorker`,
`ShadowProductionReplayService`, `ExecutionReplayScope`,
`TradeExecutionValidationService`, `DynamicProfitLockService`,
`LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, and
`PaperTradingService`.

Close-message lineage, deployed JAR/config identity and historical
order-book/derivative/sentiment availability are not durable for all old
windows. Strict claims are therefore unreachable for such eras unless a future
approved forward-capture design is implemented.

In scope: single-symbol virtual simulation; qualifier/refusal; closed-candle
identity; virtual settings/constraints; stable Replay IDs; pure policy ports;
staging; resource limits; tests. Out: Production strategy change, real exchange,
Production account reads, multi-symbol scheduling, and retirement of
FIX-11K/FIX-11Q/pre-FIX122/EXPERIMENTAL. Those paths remain explicit
`EXPERIMENTAL` and cannot enter baseline output.

## 3. Read/write boundary

| Class | Data |
|---|---|
| Forbidden Production account state | `wallet_asset`, `wallet_trade`, `wallet_managed_position`, `paper_position`, `wallet_settings`, account snapshots and daily account statistics. |
| Permitted read-only evidence | `candle`, `trade_signal`, `market_price_event`, `signal_processing_work`, `candle_analysis_outcome`, `order_book_snapshot`, `fix122_evaluation`, `buy_htf_assessment_audit`, `execution_opportunity`, subject to qualifier as-of rules. |
| Replay-owned writes | `regression_test_run`, `analysis_test_signal`, `wallet_execution_test`, `wallet_position_test`, and new RPR6 profile/certificate/staging objects. |

`NON_CANONICAL` and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` observations are deliberate
exclusions. Required accepted observations are `LEGACY`/`APPLIED`, retaining id
and ordering slot.

## 4. Complete class/file registry

| ID | Artifact | Change | Production impact |
|---|---|---|---|
| RPR6-01 | `RegressionTestWorker` | qualifier before staging; closed identity timeline | none |
| RPR6-02 | `ReplayEvidenceQualifier` new | certificate/mode/refusal | none |
| RPR6-03 | profile/store new | immutable virtual account/config | none |
| RPR6-04 | virtual-account adapter new | balances/positions/effects | none |
| RPR6-05 | `ExecutionReplayScope` | stable run keys, clocks, finally cleanup | shared; no non-Replay behavior change |
| RPR6-06 | `ShadowProductionReplayService` | staging, truthful labels, ordered calls | none |
| RPR6-07 | `TradeExecutionValidationService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
| RPR6-08 | `DynamicProfitLockService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
| RPR6-09 | `LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, `PaperTradingService` | expose pure ordered inputs, no policy reorder | shared; transaction trace/regression |
| RPR6-10 | `PositionExitPolicy`, `ProfitLockPolicy`, `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy` | virtual constraints, no Replay HTTP | shared; existing Production provider unchanged |
| RPR6-11 | `OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver` | experimental profile port and label | Replay only |
| RPR6-12 | `RegimeStateService` | guaranteed Replay cleanup | shared; no Production behavior change |
| RPR6-13 | `ReplayParityValidator` | analysis-only oracle | Replay only |
| RPR6-14 | next verified Flyway migration | profile/certificate/staging/trace schema | additive only |
| RPR6-15 | typed replay configuration | explicit modes and resource bounds | runtime protection |
| RPR6-16 | tests | A8 suite | none |
| RPR6-17 | docs | A10 final docs | none |

## 5. Method contract

| ID | Area | Required behavior |
|---|---|---|
| M01 | worker start | qualifier replaces the later pending-evidence write guard; it runs before any Replay business write. |
| M02 | timeline/snapshot | require `closed=1` and exact symbol/TF/open/close identity; mismatch refuses before staging. |
| M03 | context | select stored as-of evidence, historical recomputation, or refusal per dependency; never fallback to future data. |
| M04 | staging | no skip semantics. Any mid-run invariant/error discards staged artifacts and records only terminal run failure. |
| M05 | profile port | wallet/account/HTTP access in Replay throws named forbidden-dependency failure before publication. |
| M06 | order | price then signal path: advisory stop, TP without extension, label-free lock, rebase/floor, SELL/add suppression, progressive add. Simulation records price assumptions. |
| M07 | virtual budget | virtual daily-check exception ends `FAILED_PROFILE_EVALUATION`; never consults Production account data. |
| M08 | scope | clear Replay and `RegimeStateService` state in `finally` for success, cancel and error. |

## 6. Database/configuration/logging

| ID | Item | Requirement |
|---|---|---|
| D01 | migration | allocate next free migration only after read-only Flyway-history preflight; never edit V103/V106Г?"V109. |
| D02 | MySQL validation | prove baseline/upgrade; V103 collector dependency must be explicit, never silently satisfied. |
| C01 | `replay.default-mode` | `PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`. |
| C02 | `replay.max-concurrent-runs` | `1`. |
| C03 | `replay.profile.required` | `true`; no wallet-settings fallback. |
| C04 | executor/pool limit | bounded; reject before harming Production freshness. |
| L01 | INFO `REPLAY_QUALIFIED` | run/mode/baseline/config/evidence counts. |
| L02 | INFO `REPLAY_REFUSED` | run/requested mode/reason codes. |
| L03 | DEBUG `REPLAY_DECISION` | run/key/route/result/evidence IDs/order/clocks. |
| L04 | WARN `REPLAY_FORBIDDEN_DEPENDENCY` | component/dependency type/run, no secret. |
| L05 | INFO `REPLAY_COMPLETED` | earned mode/rows/duration/resources. |

## 7. Trading behavior

Production policy is unchanged. ReplayГ?Ts entry/exit/add/TP/lock/stop behavior
changes only by calling the same extracted pure policies with virtual account
data. Fees/slippage are profile data and zero-fee remains explicitly labelled
when selected. Historical-only/skipped work is reproduced from durable outcome
evidence or analysis parity is refused. Same-boundary higher-timeframe
visibility requires recorded/inferred ordering; otherwise strict analysis claim
is refused.

## 8. Tests and validation

| ID | Scenario | Expected result |
|---|---|---|
| X01 | open/identity-mismatched candle | refusal before output |
| X02 | missing context/config/lineage | analysis claim refused; simulation only with declared inputs |
| X03 | excluded price event status | no false evidence failure |
| X04 | account or HTTP port | fail before publication |
| X05 | Replay signal key | stable and never equal to Production ID; near-TP/consumption works |
| X06 | Production policy fixtures | exact ordered stop/TP/lock/floor/SELL/add outcomes |
| X07 | virtual budget exception | named failed run, no account read |
| X08 | experimental observer | baseline never invokes; output labelled experimental |
| X09 | cancellation/error | state cleanup and staged artifact discard |
| X10 | concurrent Replay/load | one-run/pool bounds preserve freshness |
| X11 | real MySQL migration | next migration, B105/V103 policy and upgrade path proven |
| X12 | baseline hash | CI rejects mismatched source/config request |

## 9. Risks and rollback

Risks: policy drift through shared extraction, latency, look-ahead, Flyway
collision and false labels. Mitigate with pure ports, Production regression and
transaction tests, qualification refusal, preflight and mode metrics. Rollback
disables the new Replay route; it never falls back to wallet reads. Schema is
additive and corrected only forward.

## 10. Documentation and reconciliation

After approved implementation and validation only, update relevant
`codex-docs` and `claude-docs` architecture/changelog/trading/investigation
documents. Shared knowledge/decisions change only through dual agreement.
Implementation report must map every RPR6/M/D/C/L/X item and every actual file
to this registry; any missing or unregistered material work rejects acceptance.

# Trade Investigation Agent

## Role

Investigate one Crypto AI Trader trade from durable evidence. Explain what
happened, why the system entered and exited, whether it followed the intended
rules, and whether the entry/exit was supported by the information available
at that time.

Investigations are read-only. Do not change code, configuration, services,
database records, review states, Replay work, or deployment state.

## Required input

Obtain or resolve:

- Environment and application: local, test, or production.
- Database host, port, and schema.
- One trade reference: `paper_position.id`, `wallet_trade.id`, or a symbol plus
  a bounded approximate entry-time window.
- Optional question or suspected issue.

Before analysis, confirm the symbol, trade/position ID, running version or FIX
level, and ingestion mode. Read the actual schema before choosing tables or
columns. Do not assume a table is in `crypto_ai` when it may belong to
`crypto_ai_v2`.

## Evidence rules

- Use configured database and SSH/log connections without displaying secrets.
- Use bounded, read-only queries and targeted log searches.
- Treat stored timestamps as UTC; display both UTC and KSA (`UTC+03:00`).
- Attach record IDs and timestamped log evidence to every factual conclusion.
- Distinguish **confirmed fact**, **supported inference**, and **missing or
  conflicting evidence**.
- Missing logs or records do not prove that no execution/effect occurred.
- A saved signal does not prove execution. A callback return does not prove a
  transaction committed. Under FIX-153, `market_price_event.APPLIED` proves a
  committed successful protection callback, but can be a no-op when there was
  no open position; find the position/trade mutation separately.
- Current configuration does not prove which settings were active at entry. If
  no configuration history exists, report that limitation.

## Investigation workflow

### 1. Resolve identity and environment

Link the trade reference to, where available:

- `paper_position`
- `wallet_trade`
- entry and exit `trade_signal`
- `signal_processing_work`
- wallet/position execution records

Record entry and exit times in UTC and KSA. Confirm whether the trader ran in
local-candle OFF mode or a legacy shared-market mode.

### 2. Collect a small evidence bundle

Start with exact IDs and a narrow time window. Expand only when the evidence
requires it. Collect:

| Evidence | Establishes |
| --- | --- |
| `paper_position` | lifecycle, entry/exit, stop, target, status |
| `wallet_trade` | executed quantity, prices, fees, linked signal |
| `trade_signal` | decision, strategy, scores, entry eligibility, reasons |
| `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempt, completion, expiry, uncertainty |
| `candle` / `technical_indicator` | market data and analysis inputs available then |
| `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and outcomes |
| configuration/version evidence | applicable rules, when historically available |
| application and timing logs | analysis, execution, protection, exceptions, waits |

Avoid fetching large signal explanation fields until they are needed.

### 3. Reconstruct the timeline

Build a timeline, explicitly showing missing stages:

`candle closed ƒ+' signal saved ƒ+' work claimed ƒ+' locks acquired ƒ+' entry committed ƒ+' protection observations ƒ+' exit decision ƒ+' exit committed`

Measure separately:

- candle close to signal generation;
- signal generation to processing/entry;
- observation time to protection completion;
- exit-trigger evidence to recorded exit.

Correlate delays only with logs/events for the same symbol, position, signal,
or price-event identity. A nearby slow SQL query is not proof of causation.

### 4. Assess entry correctness and quality

Use only evidence that was available at entry. Check:

- signal mode, closed-candle identity, freshness, and data quality;
- final decision and entry-allowed result;
- BTC context, higher-timeframe, ATR, liquidity, risk/reward, sizing, and
  strategy gates that applied at that time;
- correct signal/position linkage and absence of duplicate/stale-retry entry;
- quantity, wallet balance, risk limits, fees, and fill/reference-price gap.

Return two separate conclusions:

- **Execution correctness:** followed or violated the implemented rules.
- **Entry quality:** supported, weak, or unverifiable from contemporaneous
  evidence.

Subsequent price movement may evaluate the outcome; it must never be presented
as information the entry decision had.

### 5. Reconstruct protection and stop-loss behavior

For FIX-153, inspect accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events and retain
`PENDING`, `NON_CANONICAL`, and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` records as evidence
gaps or exclusions. Never substitute candle OHLC for absent intra-candle price
evidence.

For each relevant accepted observation:

1. Determine the then-active stop, target, trailing stop, and profit-lock state.
2. Find the first accepted observation that met an exit threshold.
3. Confirm that protection evaluated it and identify the resulting position or
   wallet execution record.
4. Explain delays, rejections, stale/out-of-order classification, exceptions,
   lock waits, or pool pressure.

Classify the exit path accurately: fixed stop-loss, trailing stop, profit-lock,
take-profit, signal-driven exit, manual exit, or unknown.

### 6. Assess exit correctness and quality

Compare the recorded exit reason, quantity, and price with trigger evidence.
Check threshold reach, accepted-observation identity, protection timing, and
the committed execution. Calculate maximum favourable/adverse movement during
the holding period separately from the contemporaneous exit assessment.

### 7. Reconcile financial results

Recalculate using execution records:

- bought/sold quantities and weighted entry/exit prices;
- gross P&L, fees, and net P&L;
- partial exits, remaining quantity, and realized versus unrealized values.

Flag discrepancies among wallet records, position records, and UI output.

### 8. Diagnose cause and use Replay carefully

Classify findings as one or more of:

- strategy decision;
- data freshness or coverage;
- analysis delay;
- execution failure;
- protection failure;
- database, lock, or connection-pool contention;
- accounting/UI discrepancy;
- insufficient evidence.

Run Replay only as a controlled comparison with equivalent candles, accepted
prices, strategy version, and settings. State every unavailable input or
material difference. Never claim parity by substituting candle OHLC for missing
price evidence or treating `PENDING` as accepted.

## Required final report

1. **Trade identity and environment** ƒ?" IDs, symbol, version/mode, scope.
2. **UTC/KSA timeline** ƒ?" evidence-backed sequence and latency breakdown.
3. **Entry** ƒ?" execution correctness and entry quality.
4. **Exit** ƒ?" execution correctness and exit quality.
5. **Stop-loss/profit-lock** ƒ?" worked, failed, or unverifiable, with evidence.
6. **P&L reconciliation** ƒ?" quantities, prices, fees, and discrepancies.
7. **Root cause and confidence** ƒ?" confirmed defect, valid loss, configuration
   issue, delay, or insufficient evidence.
8. **Evidence references** ƒ?" record IDs, query/log timestamps, code/config
   references.
9. **Missing evidence** ƒ?" exactly what prevents a stronger conclusion.
10. **Recommended correction and regression checks** ƒ?" only when justified.

A profitable trade can violate the rules, and a losing trade can be a correct
execution. State those independently.

# AGREED KNOWLEDGE

This document contains persistent technical knowledge explicitly agreed by
BOTH Codex and Claude. Existing and future entries must pass the dual-agent
knowledge gate in `agent-docs/COORDINATOR.md`.

## Rules
- Both agents must approve the same exact candidate text.
- No majority voting.
- Unresolved findings, rejected hypotheses and speculation do not belong here.
- Proposed architecture is not current architecture.
- Current implementation/runtime evidence has priority when contradictions arise.
- Superseded knowledge is retained with traceability rather than silently erased.

---

No agreed entries recorded yet.

# APPROVED TEAM DECISIONS

Persistent record of technical/design decisions that passed the applicable
Codex + Claude review gate and user approval gate. Preserve superseded decisions
for traceability; do not silently rewrite history.

  661:                     }
  662: 
  663:                     // FIX-069: Persist the exact production TradeSignal shape as replay output.
  664:                     // analysis_test_signal remains for backward-compatible diagnostics, while
  665:                     // trade_signal_test is the canonical parity table used for field-by-field comparison.
  666:                     // FIX-112D: Replay lineage uses exact market-candle identity, never
  667:                     // generated_at or nearest-time matching. A legitimate Replay-only
  668:                     // signal remains NULL. This metadata lookup cannot alter Production.
  669:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();

  690:             } catch (Exception exception) {

  696:                         runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), exception);
  697:             }

  699:             if (index % 10 == 0 && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
  700:                 int progress = 43 + (int) Math.round(30d * index / timeline.size());
  701:                 long progressStartedNs = System.nanoTime();

  703:                     updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", Math.min(73, progress),

  706:                     profiler.recordProgressUpdate(System.nanoTime() - progressStartedNs);
  707:                 }
  708:             }
  709:         }

  711:         } finally {
  712:             // FIX-11M must never turn a valid Replay into ERROR if diagnostic summary logging fails.
  713:             try {
  714:                 analysisService.finishReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
  715:             } catch (Exception profilingException) {

  717:                         runId, profilingException.getMessage());

  725:     private void saveGenerationError(long runId, String symbol, String interval, Candle candle, String error) {
  726:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
  727:                 INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
  728:                     (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
  729:                      generated_at, latest_price, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
  730:                 VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?)
  731:                 """, runId, symbol, interval, Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
  732:                 Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval))),
  733:                 candle.getClosePrice(), error);
  734:     }

  736:     /**
  737:      * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
  738:      * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
  739:      * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
  740:      */
  741:     private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
  742:         try {
  743:             java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
  744:             java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
  745:             columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
  746:             // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
  747:             // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
  748:             // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
  749:             columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
  750:             columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
  751:             columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);
  752: 
  753:             for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
  754:                 if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
  755:                 Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
  756:                 if (column == null) continue;
  757:                 field.setAccessible(true);
  758:                 Object value = field.get(signal);
  759:                 if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
  760:                 if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
  761:                 String columnName = column.name();
  762:                 if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
  763:                 columns.add(columnName);
  764:                 values.add(value);
  765:             }
  766: 
  767:             String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
  768:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
  769:         } catch (Exception e) {
  770:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
  771:         }
  772:     }
  773: 
  774:     private String snakeCase(String value) {
  775:         return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
  776:     }
  777: 
  778:     private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
  779:         return switch (interval) {
  780:             case "1m" -> 60L;
  781:             case "5m" -> 300L;
  782:             case "1h" -> 3600L;
  783:             default -> 60L;
  784:         };
  785:     }
  786: 
  787:     private int intervalOrder(String interval) {
  788:         return switch (interval) {
  789:             case "1h" -> 0;
  790:             case "5m" -> 1;
  791:             default -> 2;
  792:         };
  793:     }
  794:

  797:     }

  562:     private ExitDecision evaluateExit(long runId, ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s, TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
  563:         BigDecimal price = s.getLatestPrice();
  564:         if (price == null) return ExitDecision.hold();
  565: 
  566:         // Replay uses the exact same temporal price-authority rule as production. A signal
  567:         // generated later may still carry a candle close from before entry; that historical
  568:         // price may update MTF context but may not retroactively hit TP/SL/profit-lock.
  569:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime());
  570:         if (authoritativePrice && p.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(p.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
  571:             // Exact production TP-continuation call, including immutable entry structure.
  572:             // FIX-011 regression relies on Replay and live using this same shared policy.
  573:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
  574:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
  575:                     p.entryTrend(), p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(),
  576:                     p.entryConfidence(), p.entryScore());
  577:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
  578:                 BigDecimal distance = p.takeProfit().subtract(p.entryPrice());
  579:                 BigDecimal newTarget = p.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
  580:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", p.takeProfit(), newTarget, p, continuation.explanation());
  581:                 jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, p.positionId());
  582:                 return new ExitDecision(false, "EXTEND_TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), newTarget);
  583:             }
  584:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
  585:                     runId, s.getSymbol(), p.positionId(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
  586:                     p.entryTime(), p.entryPrice(), p.quantity(), p.cost(), p.stopLoss(), p.takeProfit(), p.highest(),
  587:                     p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.entryScore(), p.entryConfidence(), p.entryTrend(),
  588:                     p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(), oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
  589:                     continuation.explanation());
  590:             persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), p, continuation.explanation());
  591:             return new ExitDecision(true, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation());
  592:         }
  593:         // FIX-052 fallback ordering also mirrors Production: STOP_LOSS is absolute and
  594:         // is evaluated before profit-lock. Exact V64 runs normally take this path from
  595:         // persisted live-price events rather than from signal-carried prices.
  596:         if (authoritativePrice && p.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(p.stopLoss()) <= 0)
  597:             return new ExitDecision(true, "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the stored stop loss.");
  598: 
  599:         ShadowPosition updated = authoritativePrice ? profitLockState(p, price) : p;
  600:         if (authoritativePrice && (updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE
  601:                 || updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
  602:                 || p.profitLockActive() != updated.profitLockActive()
  603:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice())
  604:                 || p.profitLockState() != updated.profitLockState())) {
  605:             BigDecimal fix118Progress = updated.takeProfit() == null || updated.takeProfit().compareTo(updated.entryPrice()) <= 0
  606:                     ? BigDecimal.ZERO
  607:                     : updated.highest().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC)
  608:                             .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
  609:                             .divide(updated.takeProfit().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
  610:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_PATH] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, " +
  611:                             "entry={}, target={}, highest={}, previousActive={}, active={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousLock={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, triggered={}",
  612:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
  613:                     updated.entryPrice(), updated.takeProfit(), updated.highest(), p.profitLockActive(), updated.profitLockActive(),
  614:                     p.profitLockState(), updated.profitLockState(), p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice(), fix118Progress,
  615:                     updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
  616:                             && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0);
  617:         }
  618:         BigDecimal minimumProfitableExit = p.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005));
  619:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
  620:                 && price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
  621:             String floorReason = "TP-extension rebase safety floor was breached at " + minimumProfitableExit + ". "
  622:                     + profitLockConfigText();
  623:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, floor={}",
  624:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price, minimumProfitableExit);
  625:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
  626:             return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
  627:         }
  628:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
  629:                 && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
  630:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, lock={}, target={}",
  631:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
  632:                     updated.profitLockPrice(), updated.takeProfit());
  633:             if (price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
  634:                 String floorReason = "Profit-lock hard floor was breached; protected profit can no longer be preserved. "
  635:                         + profitLockConfigText();
  636:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
  637:                 return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
  638:             }
  639:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(
  640:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
  641:             if (!lockDecision.exit()) {
  642:                 String holdReason = lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
  643:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, holdReason);
  644:                 return ExitDecision.hold();
  645:             }
  646:             String lockReason = "Price retraced to the protected profit level after a profitable advance. "
  647:                     + lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
  648:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, lockReason);
  649:             return new ExitDecision(true, lockDecision.code(), lockReason);
  650:         }
  651:         // Production has two SELL authorities: the live HTF PositionExitPolicy and the
  652:         // 1m signal TradeExecutionValidationService. Replay executes both shared production
  653:         // policies in the same order instead of carrying a test-only SELL rule.
  654:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(
  655:                 oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
  656:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
  657:             return new ExitDecision(true, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation());
  658:         }
  659:         if ("1m".equals(s.getInterval()) && bearish(s.getDecision())) {
  660:             TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(s);
  661:             if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
  662:                 return new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
  663:             }
  664:         }
  665:         return ExitDecision.hold();
  666:     }
  667: 
  668:     private FallbackNearTpEvaluation evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(long runId, ShadowPosition current,
  669:                                                                     TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal oneMinute,
  670:                                                                     TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
  671:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
  672:                 current.nearTpState(), current.nearTpBestPrice(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(),
  673:                 current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), current.nearTpHarvestUsed());
  674:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
  675:                 state, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt(),
  676:                 oneMinute, fiveMinute);
  677:         ShadowPosition updated = current.withNearTp(evaluation.state());
  678:         if (!updated.sameNearTp(current)) persistNearTpState(updated);
  679:         if (evaluation.transition()) {
  680:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback Near-TP: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, state={}, price={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, detail={}",
  681:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), updated.nearTpState(),
  682:                     signal.getLatestPrice(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), updated.nearTpBearishStreak(), evaluation.explanation());
  683:         }
  684:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
  685: 
  686:         BigDecimal soldQty = updated.quantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
  687:         BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.Evaluation minimumCheck =
  688:                 binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, signal.getLatestPrice());
  689:         if (!minimumCheck.executable()) {
  690:             updated = updated.withNearTp(new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
  691:                     NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, updated.nearTpBestPrice(),
  692:                     0, updated.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
  693:             persistNearTpState(updated);
  694:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",
  695:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
  696:                     minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
  697:             return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
  698:         }
  699:         BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(signal.getLatestPrice(), MC);
  700:         BigDecimal soldCost = updated.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
  701:         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
  702:         BigDecimal pnlPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : pnl.divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
  703:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
  704:         updated = updated.withPartialHarvest(soldQty, soldCost, pnl);
  705:         persistNearTpPartialHarvestAtSignal(runId, signal, updated, soldQty, proceeds, pnl, pnlPct, evaluation.explanation());
  706:         log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, price={}, partialPnl={}",
  707:                 runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, updated.quantity(), signal.getLatestPrice(), pnl);
  708:         return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, proceeds, pnl);
  709:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java 1-160 =====
    1: package com.crypto.regression.service;
    2: 
    3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
    4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
    5: 
    6: import java.util.ArrayList;
    7: import java.util.List;
    8: import java.util.Objects;
    9: 
   10: /**
   11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
   12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
   13:  */
   14: @Service
   15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
   16: 
   17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
   18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
   19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
   20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
   21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
   22:         }
   23: 
   24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
   25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
   26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
   27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
   28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
   29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
   30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
   31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
   32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
   33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
   34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
   35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
   36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
   37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
   38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
   39:     }
   40: 
   41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
   42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
   43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
   44:         }
   45:     }
   46: 
   47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
   48: }

  178:     public synchronized long start(RegressionTestRunRequest request, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
  179:         if (request == null || request.symbol() == null || request.symbol().isBlank()
  180:                 || request.startTime() == null || request.endTime() == null
  181:                 || !request.endTime().isAfter(request.startTime())) {
  182:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol, start time and end time are required, and end must be after start.");
  183:         }
  184: 
  185:         Integer activeRuns = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
  186:                 SELECT COUNT(*)
  187:                 FROM analysis_test_run
  188:                 WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
  189:                 """, Integer.class);
  190:         if (activeRuns != null && activeRuns > 0) {
  191:             Map<String, Object> active = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap("""
  192:                     SELECT id, test_name, symbol, status, progress_percent, started_at, created_at
  193:                     FROM analysis_test_run
  194:                     WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
  195:                     ORDER BY id DESC
  196:                     LIMIT 1
  197:                     """);
  198:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
  199:                     "Regression test #" + active.get("id") + " is already " + active.get("status")
  200:                             + " for " + active.get("symbol") + ". Wait for it to finish before starting another run.");
  201:         }
  202: 
  203:         String symbol = request.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  204:         ReplayDataSource effectiveSource = dataSource == null ? ReplayDataSource.DATABASE : dataSource;
  205:         String baseTestName = request.testName() == null || request.testName().isBlank()
  206:                 ? symbol + " regression" : request.testName().trim();
  207:         String testName = (baseTestName + " [" + effectiveSource.name() + "]").substring(
  208:                 0, Math.min(180, baseTestName.length() + effectiveSource.name().length() + 3));
  209: 
  210:         KeyHolder keyHolder = new GeneratedKeyHolder();
  211:         jdbcTemplate.update(connection -> {
  212:             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement("""
  213:                     INSERT INTO analysis_test_run
  214:                         (test_name, symbol, start_time, end_time, status, progress_percent, current_step)
  215:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'PENDING', 0, 'Queued from Administration')
  216:                     """, Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);
  217:             statement.setString(1, testName);
  218:             statement.setString(2, symbol);
  219:             statement.setTimestamp(3, Timestamp.from(request.startTime()));
  220:             statement.setTimestamp(4, Timestamp.from(request.endTime()));
  221:             return statement;
  222:         }, keyHolder);
  223: 
  224:         Number key = keyHolder.getKey();
  225:         if (key == null) {
  226:             throw new IllegalStateException("Could not create regression test run.");
  227:         }
  228:         long id = key.longValue();
  229:         // FIX-122: persist the business-rule revision separately from data-source/experimental modes.
  230:         jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO fix122_replay_revision(test_run_id,enabled) VALUES (?,?)",
  231:                 id, !Boolean.FALSE.equals(request.fix122Enabled()));
  232:         worker.runAsync(id, effectiveSource);
  233:         return id;
  234:     }

  237:     /**
  238:      * Archives one complete replay run before it can be cleared from the active test tables.
  239:      *
  240:      * IMPORTANT regression-history rule (introduced after the ETHUSDT Trade #2 investigation,
  241:      * Aug-2026): test rows are evidence. We must be able to rerun changed production logic without
  242:      * destroying the old pipeline/evidence/health/trade trace that still needs manual analysis.
  243:      * Proven trades remain separate in proven_analyzed_trade and are never moved or deleted here.
  244:      */
  245:     @Transactional
  246:     public synchronized Map<String, Object> archiveRun(long runId, String reason) {
  247:         Integer active = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=? AND status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')", Integer.class, runId);
  248:         if (active != null && active > 0) {
  249:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Test #" + runId + " is still running and cannot be archived.");
  250:         }
  251:         List<Map<String,Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id,test_name,symbol,start_time,end_time,status FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=?", runId);
  252:         if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Regression test #" + runId + " not found.");
  253:         List<Map<String,Object>> existing = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id FROM regression_test_archive_batch WHERE source_test_run_id=?", runId);
  254:         if (!existing.isEmpty()) return Map.of("archiveBatchId", existing.get(0).get("id"), "sourceTestRunId", runId, "alreadyArchived", true);
  255:         Map<String,Object> r = rows.get(0);
  256:         KeyHolder kh = new GeneratedKeyHolder();
  257:         jdbcTemplate.update(c -> {
  258:             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement("INSERT INTO regression_test_archive_batch(source_test_run_id,test_name,symbol,start_time,end_time,source_status,archive_reason) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)", Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);
  259:             ps.setLong(1, runId); ps.setString(2, String.valueOf(r.get("test_name"))); ps.setString(3, String.valueOf(r.get("symbol")));
  260:             ps.setTimestamp(4, (Timestamp) r.get("start_time")); ps.setTimestamp(5, (Timestamp) r.get("end_time"));
  261:             ps.setString(6, String.valueOf(r.get("status"))); ps.setString(7, reason == null || reason.isBlank() ? "Archived before replay reset" : reason.trim());
  262:             return ps;
  263:         }, kh);
  264:         long batchId = kh.getKey().longValue();
  265:         int runs = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO analysis_test_run_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM analysis_test_run r WHERE r.id=?", batchId, runId);
  266:         int signals = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM analysis_test_signal r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
  267:         // FIX-069: archive the full production-shaped replay signal snapshot as well.
  268:         int fullTradeSignals = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM trade_signal_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
  269:         int opportunities = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO execution_opportunity_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM execution_opportunity_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
  270:         int results = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO analysis_test_result_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM analysis_test_result r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
  271:         int executions = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO wallet_execution_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM wallet_execution_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
  272:         int positions = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO wallet_position_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM wallet_position_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
  273:         int management = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO position_management_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM position_management_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
  274:         int defensiveObservations = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
  275:         int continuationGraceObservations = jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO one_candle_continuation_grace_test_archive SELECT ?, r.* FROM one_candle_continuation_grace_test r WHERE r.test_run_id=?", batchId, runId);
  276:         return Map.ofEntries(
  277:                 Map.entry("archiveBatchId", batchId), Map.entry("sourceTestRunId", runId), Map.entry("alreadyArchived", false),
  278:                 Map.entry("runs", runs), Map.entry("signals", signals), Map.entry("tradeSignals", fullTradeSignals),

  105:     /** FIX-127: existing decision order; invoked inside the coordinator's transaction. */
  106:     private Optional<PaperPosition> processSignalBody(TradeSignal signal) {
  107:         if (signal == null) {
  108:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Trade signal is required");
  109:         }
  110: 
  111:         String symbol = normalizeSymbol(signal.getSymbol());
  112: 
  113:         // FIX-127: position management persists advice and can request POSITION_STOP_LOSS.
  114:         // Keep its existing decision order, but acquire the initial managed lock before entering here.
  115:         if (positionManagementService != null) {
  116:             try {
  117:                 positionManagementService.analyze(signal);
  118:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  119:                 log.warn("Position advisory failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage());
  120:             }
  121:         }
  122:         Optional<PaperPosition> openPosition = positionRepository
  123:                 .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN);
  124: 
  125:         if (openPosition.isPresent()) {
  126:             PaperPosition position = openPosition.get();
  127:             BigDecimal price = signal.getLatestPrice();
  128: 
  129:             // Never treat a delayed timeframe candle close as the current mark price.
  130:             // Context from that signal is still processed below, but TP/SL/profit-lock
  131:             // mechanics require a post-entry market observation. Live production protection
  132:             // is independently driven by LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice(...).
  133:             boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, position.getOpenedAt());
  134:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock = authoritativePrice
  135:                     ? dynamicProfitLockService.evaluate(signal)
  136:                     : DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation.inactive(
  137:                             "Signal price predates the open position and is context-only for mechanical protection.");
  138: 
  139:             // FIX-118 Production trace for the independent signal-price Profit Lock authority.
  140:             // The explicit ProfitLockState now also shows whether the historical lock is ACTIVE
  141:             // or temporarily non-executable while TP extension is rebasing.
  142:             if (authoritativePrice && (profitLock.active() || profitLock.triggered() || profitLock.rebasing())) {
  143:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_PATH] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, price={}, " +
  144:                                 "active={}, progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
  145:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, signal.getInterval(), price,
  146:                         profitLock.active(), profitLock.progressPercent(), profitLock.activationPercent(),
  147:                         profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.state(), profitLock.triggered());
  148:             }
  149: 
  150:             // FIX-067: The wallet-managed position is the authoritative Production source for
  151:             // a dynamically extended take-profit.  paper_position historically retained the
  152:             // entry-time TP, so a fresh 1m signal could close against that stale threshold even
  153:             // after LivePositionProtectionService had persisted TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED.
  154:             // Prefer the OPEN managed TP and repair the paper snapshot when they differ. This is
  155:             // deliberately NOT a TP-policy change: it only makes both existing exit paths compare
  156:             // against the same already-approved target.
  157:             BigDecimal authoritativeTakeProfit = position.getTakeProfit();
  158:             WalletManagedPosition managedPosition = walletManagedPositionRepository
  159:                     .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  160:                     .orElse(null);
  161:             if (managedPosition != null && managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null) {
  162:                 authoritativeTakeProfit = managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt();
  163:                 if (position.getTakeProfit() == null
  164:                         || position.getTakeProfit().compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) != 0) {
  165:                     position.setTakeProfit(authoritativeTakeProfit);
  166:                     positionRepository.save(position);
  167:                 }
  168:             }
  169: 
  170:             if (authoritativePrice && authoritativeTakeProfit != null
  171:                     && price.compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) >= 0) {
  172:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
  173:                         "TAKE_PROFIT", "Price reached the authoritative configured take-profit target."));
  174:             }
  175: 
  176:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.triggered()) {
  177:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}",
  178:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.progressPercent());
  179:                 return Optional.of(closeFromProfitLock(position, signal, profitLock));
  180:             }
  181: 
  182:             if (authoritativePrice && price.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0) {
  183:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.STOPPED,
  184:                         "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the configured stop loss."));
  185:             }
  186: 
  187:             // FIX-118: in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the old Profit Lock cannot execute, but the
  188:             // direct protected-profit floor still applies. Stop Loss remains absolute and
  189:             // therefore keeps priority immediately above this branch.
  190:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
  191:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}",
  192:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.hardProfitFloor());
  193:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
  194:                         "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", profitLock.explanation()));
  195:             }
  196: 
  197:             if (signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.SELL
  198:                     || signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
  199:                 if (profitLock.active()) {
  200:                     log.info("Normal wallet SELL suppressed because Dynamic Profit Lock is active: signalId={}, symbol={}, protectedPrice={}, currentPrice={}",
  201:                             signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), profitLock.lockPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
  202:                     return Optional.of(position);
  203:                 }
  204:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validation =
  205:                         executionValidationService.validateSell(signal);
  206:                 if (validation.allowed()) {
  207:                     return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
  208:                             signal.getDecision().name(), signal.getExplanation()));
  209:                 }
  210:                 log.info("Normal wallet SELL rejected: signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, reason={}, detail={}",
  211:                         signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(),
  212:                         validation.code(), validation.explanation());
  213:             }
  214: 
  215:             // Progressive Position Building may add to an existing position only after
  216:             // TP / Profit Lock / SL / validated SELL logic had first priority.
  217:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && !profitLock.active()) {
  218:                 WalletManagedPosition managed = walletManagedPositionRepository
  219:                         .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  220:                         .orElse(null);
  221:                 if (managed != null) {

  223:                             executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(
  224:                                     signal,
  225:                                     managed.getAllocatedPositionPercent(),
  226:                                     managed.getEntryStage());
  227:                     if (addDecision.allowed()) {
  228:                         if (walletTradeRepository
  229:                                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
  230:                                         signal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")

  413:     private ValidationResult cap(ValidationResult result, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
  414:         if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
  415:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
  416:         if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();
  417:         return capped==result.positionPercent()?result:ValidationResult.allow(capped,result.code(),result.explanation()+" FIX-154 soft-HTF cap="+capped+"%.");
  418:     }

  420:     /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
  421:     public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
  422:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
  423:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  424:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  425:         if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
  426:                 || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
  427:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
  428:         if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
  429:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softAddBlocked();
  430:             audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",
  431:                     "Existing raw 1h progressive-add guard blocked this add; FIX-154 audit only.");
  432:         }
  433:     }
  434: 
  435:     private void audit(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String path,
  436:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment, String finalResult, String reason) {
  437:         if (htfAuditStore == null) return;
  438:         try {
  439:             htfAuditStore.record(referenceSignal, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
  440:         } catch (RuntimeException auditFailure) {
  441:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  442:             log.warn("FIX-154 audit handoff failed; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, signalId={}",
  443:                     path, referenceSignal == null ? null : referenceSignal.getId(), auditFailure);
  444:         }
  445:     }

  449:                 && assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.NOT_TRUSTED) {
  450:             fix154Metrics.untrustedBearishOneHourBlocked();
  451:         }
  452:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java 1-140 =====
    1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
    2: 
    3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
    4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
    5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
    6: 
    7: import java.time.Instant;
    8: import java.util.ArrayList;
    9: import java.util.Comparator;
   10: import java.util.List;
   11: import java.util.Optional;
   12: import java.util.HashSet;
   13: import java.util.Set;
   14: import java.util.function.Consumer;
   15: 
   16: /**
   17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
   18:  *
   19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
   20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
   21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
   22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
   23:  */
   24: @Component
   25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
   26: 
   27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
   28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
   29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
   30: 
   31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
   32: 
   33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
   34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
   35:     }
   36: 
   37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
   38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
   39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
   40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
   41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
   42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
   43:                 .toList();
   44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
   45:         return new Scope();
   46:     }
   47: 
   48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
   49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
   50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
   51:         State s=required();
   52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
   53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
   54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
   55:     }
   56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
   57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
   58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
   59:     }
   60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
   61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
   62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
   63: 
   64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
   65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
   66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
   67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
   68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
   69: 
   70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
   71: 
   72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
   73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
   74:         State s = required();
   75:         s.reference = observedAt;
   76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
   77:     }
   78: 
   79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
   80:         State s = required();
   81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
   82:     }
   83: 
   84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
   85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
   86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
   87:     }
   88: 
   89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
   90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
   91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
   92:     }
   93: 
   94:     /**
   95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
   96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
   97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
   98:      */
   99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
  100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
  101:         State s = required();
  102:         s.signals.add(signal);
  103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
  104:     }
  105: 
  106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
  107:         State s = required();
  108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
  109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
  110:         return s.signals.stream()
  111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
  112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
  113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
  114:                 .limit(limit)
  115:                 .toList();
  116:     }
  117: 
  118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
  119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
  120:     }
  121: 
  122:     /**
  123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
  124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
  125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
  126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
  127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
  128:      */
  129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
  130:         State s = required();
  131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
  132:         return s.signals.stream()
  133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
  134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
  135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
  136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
  137:     }
  138: 
  139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
  140:         State s = required();

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java 1-130 =====
    1: package com.crypto.regression.service;
    2: 
    3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
    4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
    5: 
    6: import java.util.ArrayList;
    7: import java.util.List;
    8: import java.util.Objects;
    9: 
   10: /**
   11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
   12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
   13:  */
   14: @Service
   15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
   16: 
   17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
   18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
   19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
   20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
   21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
   22:         }
   23: 
   24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
   25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
   26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
   27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
   28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
   29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
   30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
   31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
   32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
   33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
   34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
   35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
   36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
   37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
   38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
   39:     }
   40: 
   41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
   42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
   43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
   44:         }
   45:     }
   46: 
   47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
   48: }

  238:      * Archives one complete replay run before it can be cleared from the active test tables.

  240:      * IMPORTANT regression-history rule (introduced after the ETHUSDT Trade #2 investigation,
  241:      * Aug-2026): test rows are evidence. We must be able to rerun changed production logic without

  245:     @Transactional
  246:     public synchronized Map<String, Object> archiveRun(long runId, String reason) {
  247:         Integer active = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=? AND status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')", Integer.class, runId);
  248:         if (active != null && active > 0) {
  249:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Test #" + runId + " is still running and cannot be archived.");
  250:         }
  251:         List<Map<String,Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id,test_name,symbol,start_time,end_time,status FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=?", runId);
  252:         if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Regression test #" + runId + " not found.");
  253:         List<Map<String,Object>> existing = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id FROM regression_test_archive_batch WHERE source_test_run_id=?", runId);

  262:             return ps;

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceStore.java 18-75 =====
   18: @Service
   19: public class StopLossEvidenceStore {
   20:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
   21:     private final TransactionTemplate read;
   22:     private final TransactionTemplate write;
   23: 
   24:     public StopLossEvidenceStore(DataSource dataSource, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
   25:         jdbc = new JdbcTemplate(dataSource);
   26:         jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
   27:         read = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
   28:         read.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
   29:         read.setIsolationLevel(TransactionDefinition.ISOLATION_READ_COMMITTED);
   30:         read.setReadOnly(true);
   31:         read.setTimeout(2);
   32:         write = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
   33:         write.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
   34:         write.setTimeout(2);
   35:     }
   36: 
   37:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary latest(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
   38:         return read.execute(status -> jdbc.query("""
   39:                 SELECT id, executed_at FROM wallet_trade
   40:                 WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED' AND side='SELL'
   41:                   AND execution_reason='STOP_LOSS' AND executed_at<=?
   42:                 ORDER BY executed_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1
   43:                 """, (rs, row) -> new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(rs.getLong(1),
   44:                 rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()), symbol, Timestamp.from(asOf))
   45:                 .stream().findFirst().orElse(null));
   46:     }
   47: 
   48:     public void append(Long runId, String symbol, Long signalId, Instant at, String stage, String payload) {
   49:         // Separate audit transaction survives a caller rollback; it records an evaluation,
   50:         // never asserts that a BUY/SELL actually executed. No FK to wallet rows is acquired.
   51:         write.executeWithoutResult(status -> jdbc.update("""
   52:                 INSERT INTO fix122_evaluation(test_run_id,symbol,signal_id,evaluated_at,stage,payload)
   53:                 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)
   54:                 """, runId, symbol, signalId, Timestamp.from(at), stage, payload));
   55:     }
   56: 
   57:     public List<Map<String,Object>> replayDetails(long runId) {
   58:         return jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id=? ORDER BY id", runId);
   59:     }
   60: 
   61:     public List<Map<String,Object>> productionDetails(String symbol, Instant from, Instant to) {
   62:         return jdbc.queryForList("""
   63:                 SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id IS NULL AND symbol=?
   64:                 AND evaluated_at>=? AND evaluated_at<=? ORDER BY id
   65:                 """, symbol, Timestamp.from(from), Timestamp.from(to));
   66:     }
   67: }

  140:                 LIMIT 1
  141:                 """, (rs, rowNum) -> new PriceEvent(
  142:                         rs.getTimestamp("observed_at").toInstant(),
  143:                         rs.getBigDecimal("price"), rs.getString("source")),
  144:                 symbol, Timestamp.from(reference));
  145:         return rows.stream().findFirst();
  146:     }

  149:      * become legacy candle-price fallback. Historical pre-collector windows retain
  150:      * their established fallback. This reads immutable evidence, never live claims. */
  151:     public void assertReplayEvidence(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to) {
  152:         Long pending=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",

  156:         if("OFF".equals(mode))return;

  162:         }

  156:     /**
  157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
  158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
  159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
  160:      *
  161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
  162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
  163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
  164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
  165:      */
  166:     @Transactional
  167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
  168:         if (indicator == null) {
  169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
  170:         }
  171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
  172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
  173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
  174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
  175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
  176:     }
  177: 
  178:     /**

  180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.

  183:     public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
  184:         if (snapshot == null) {
  185:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression indicator snapshot is required");
  186:         }
  187:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
  188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
  189:         try {
  190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
  191:         } finally {
  192:             if (profiler != null) {
  193:                 profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
  194:             }
  195:         }
  196:     }
  197:

  202:      */
  203:     public void beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {

  235:         boolean fundamentalAvailable = fundamentalService.isAvailable(fundamental, signalGeneratedAt);

  237:

  239:         IndicatorSnapshot previous = previousSnapshot(i);
  240:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.PREVIOUS_SNAPSHOT, profileStartedNs);
  241:

   39:                                                             boolean entryAllowed, Instant evaluatedAt) {
   40:         Instant time = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
   41:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
   42:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
   43:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
   44:                     "Derivatives positioning is disabled.", time);
   45:         }
