   51: 
   52:     public DerivativesPositioningResult evaluate(String symbol, String interval, SignalDecision decision,
   53:                                                   boolean entryAllowed, Instant evaluatedAt) {
   54:         Instant now = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
   55:         if (!properties.enabled()) return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
   56:                 null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
   57:                 "Derivatives positioning is disabled.", now);
   58:         try {
   59:             List<BinanceFundingRate> funding = client.fundingHistory(symbol, properties.fundingHistoryLimit());
   60:             List<BinanceOpenInterestPoint> oi = client.openInterestHistory(symbol, period(interval), properties.openInterestHistoryLimit());
   61:             if (funding.isEmpty() || oi.size() < 2) {
   62:                 return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.LEARNING, decision, entryAllowed,
   63:                         latestFunding(funding), percentile(funding), latestOi(oi), latestOiValue(oi), null,
   64:                         priceChange(symbol, interval), funding.size(), period(interval), -5,
   65:                         "Funding or open-interest history is still insufficient; no veto was applied.", now);
   66:             }
   67:             BigDecimal fundingRate = latestFunding(funding);
   68:             BigDecimal fundingPercentile = percentile(funding);
   69:             BigDecimal oiChange = percentChange(oi.get(oi.size() - 2).sumOpenInterest(), oi.get(oi.size() - 1).sumOpenInterest());
   70:             BigDecimal priceChange = priceChange(symbol, interval);
   71:             DerivativesPositioningStatus status = classify(fundingRate, oiChange, priceChange);
   72:             SignalDecision finalDecision = decision;
   73:             boolean allowed = entryAllowed;
   74:             int confidence = confidence(status);
   75:             if (isBullish(decision) && status == DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED
   76:                     && properties.vetoExtremeCrowding()
   77:                     && oiChange != null && oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.strongOpenInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0) {
   78:                 finalDecision = SignalDecision.WATCH;
   79:                 allowed = false;
   80:             }
   81:             if (isBearish(decision) && status == DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORTS_CROWDED
   82:                     && properties.vetoExtremeCrowding()
   83:                     && oiChange != null && oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.strongOpenInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0) {
   84:                 finalDecision = SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
   85:                 allowed = false;
   86:             }
   87:             String explanation = explanation(status, fundingRate, fundingPercentile, oiChange, priceChange, allowed);
   88:             BinanceOpenInterestPoint latest = oi.get(oi.size() - 1);
   89:             return result(status, finalDecision, allowed, fundingRate, fundingPercentile,
   90:                     latest.sumOpenInterest(), latest.sumOpenInterestValue(), oiChange, priceChange,
   91:                     funding.size(), period(interval), confidence, explanation, now);
   92:         } catch (RestClientResponseException ex) {
   93:             if (ex.getStatusCode().value() == 400) {
   94:                 return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
   95:                         null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
   96:                         "No matching Binance USD-M perpetual contract is available for " + symbol + ".", now);
   97:             }
   98:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
   99:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), -10,
  100:                     "Binance futures positioning data is unavailable: " + ex.getMessage(), now);
  101:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  102:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  103:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), -10,
  104:                     "Binance futures positioning data is unavailable: " + ex.getMessage(), now);
  105:         }
  106:     }
  107: 
  108:     private DerivativesPositioningStatus classify(BigDecimal funding, BigDecimal oiChange, BigDecimal priceChange) {
  109:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.extremeFundingRate()) >= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED;
  110:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.extremeFundingRate().negate()) <= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORTS_CROWDED;
  111:         if (oiChange == null || priceChange == null) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.BALANCED;
  112:         boolean oiRising = oiChange.compareTo(properties.openInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0;
  113:         boolean oiFalling = oiChange.compareTo(properties.openInterestChangeThreshold().negate()) <= 0;
  114:         if (priceChange.signum() > 0 && oiRising) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.FRESH_LONG_BUILDUP;
  115:         if (priceChange.signum() < 0 && oiRising) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.FRESH_SHORT_BUILDUP;
  116:         if (priceChange.signum() > 0 && oiFalling) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORT_COVERING;
  117:         if (priceChange.signum() < 0 && oiFalling) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONG_LIQUIDATION;
  118:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.moderateFundingRate()) >= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.HEALTHY_BULLISH;
  119:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.moderateFundingRate().negate()) <= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.HEALTHY_BEARISH;
  120:         return oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.openInterestChangeThreshold()) < 0
  121:                 ? DerivativesPositioningStatus.LOW_CONVICTION : DerivativesPositioningStatus.BALANCED;
  122:     }
  123: 
  124:     private int confidence(DerivativesPositioningStatus status) {
  125:         return switch (status) {
  126:             case FRESH_LONG_BUILDUP, FRESH_SHORT_BUILDUP -> 8;
  127:             case HEALTHY_BULLISH, HEALTHY_BEARISH, BALANCED -> 3;
  128:             case SHORT_COVERING, LONG_LIQUIDATION, LOW_CONVICTION -> -5;
  129:             case LONGS_CROWDED, SHORTS_CROWDED -> -15;
  130:             case LEARNING -> -5;
  131:             case UNAVAILABLE -> -10;
  132:             case NOT_APPLICABLE -> 0;
  133:         };
  134:     }
  135: 
  136:     private String explanation(DerivativesPositioningStatus status, BigDecimal funding, BigDecimal percentile,
  137:                                BigDecimal oiChange, BigDecimal priceChange, boolean allowed) {
  138:         return "Positioning " + status + "; funding=" + value(funding) + ", percentile=" + value(percentile)
  139:                 + "%, OI change=" + value(oiChange) + "%, price change=" + value(priceChange)
  140:                 + "%. Entry " + (allowed ? "remains allowed" : "was blocked") + ".";
  141:     }
  142: 
  143:     private BigDecimal priceChange(String symbol, String interval) {
  144:         List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandles(symbol, interval, PageRequest.of(0, 2)));
  145:         if (candles.size() < 2) return null;

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketContextService.java 35-105 =====
   35: 
   36:     public MarketContextSnapshot build(
   37:             IndicatorSnapshot indicator,
   38:             AtrRiskAssessment atrRisk,
   39:             SentimentOverview sentimentOverview,
   40:             boolean sentimentEnabled,
   41:             Instant evaluatedAt,
   42:             boolean historicalReplay
   43:     ) {
   44:         Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
   45:         MultiTimeframeConfluenceResult timeframe = multiTimeframeConfluenceService.evaluate(
   46:                 indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, snapshotTime);
   47:         BtcMarketContextResult btc = btcMarketContextService.evaluate(
   48:                 indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true, snapshotTime);
   49:         OrderBookLiquidityResult liquidity = historicalReplay
   50:                 ? orderBookLiquidityService.evaluateHistorical(
   51:                         indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true,
   52:                         indicator.latestPrice(), atrRisk.stopLoss(), atrRisk.takeProfit(), snapshotTime)
   53:                 : orderBookLiquidityService.evaluate(
   54:                         indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true, indicator.latestPrice(),
   55:                         atrRisk.stopLoss(), atrRisk.takeProfit(), snapshotTime);
   56:         DerivativesPositioningResult derivatives = historicalReplay
   57:                 ? derivativesPositioningService.evaluateHistorical(
   58:                         indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true, snapshotTime)
   59:                 : derivativesPositioningService.evaluate(
   60:                         indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true, snapshotTime);
   61: 
   62:         List<ProviderSentiment> providers = sentimentOverview == null || sentimentOverview.providers() == null
   63:                 ? List.of() : sentimentOverview.providers();
   64:         int enabled = (int) providers.stream().filter(ProviderSentiment::enabled).count();
   65:         int contributing = (int) providers.stream()
   66:                 .filter(ProviderSentiment::enabled)
   67:                 .filter(provider -> provider.effectiveWeight() != null && provider.effectiveWeight().signum() > 0)
   68:                 .count();
   69:         BigDecimal coverage = enabled == 0
   70:                 ? (sentimentEnabled ? BigDecimal.ZERO : BigDecimal.ONE)
   71:                 : BigDecimal.valueOf(contributing)
   72:                     .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(enabled), 4, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
   73: 
   74:         boolean valid = indicator.latestPrice() != null && indicator.latestPrice().signum() > 0
   75:                 && indicator.ema20() != null && indicator.ema50() != null && indicator.ema200() != null;
   76:         List<String> evidence = new ArrayList<>();
   77:         evidence.add("Higher-timeframe context: " + timeframe.status());
   78:         evidence.add("BTC relationship/context: " + btc.relationshipType() + " / " + btc.contextStatus());
   79:         evidence.add("Order-book liquidity: " + liquidity.status());
   80:         evidence.add("Funding/open interest: " + derivatives.status());
   81:         if (sentimentEnabled) {
   82:             evidence.add("Sentiment coverage: " + contributing + "/" + enabled + " enabled providers");
   83:         } else {
   84:             evidence.add("Sentiment is disabled and removed from the available maximum");
   85:         }
   86:         if (!valid) {
   87:             evidence.add("Required technical inputs are incomplete");
   88:         }
   89: 
   90:         return new MarketContextSnapshot(
   91:                 indicator.symbol(), indicator.intervalCode(), snapshotTime, valid,
   92:                 timeframe.status(), timeframe.higherInterval(), timeframe.higherTimeframeTrendScore(),
   93:                 btc.relationshipType(), btc.contextStatus(), btc.correlation(), btc.beta(),
   94:                 btc.influenceFactor(), btc.stable(), liquidity.status(), liquidity.imbalance(),
   95:                 liquidity.spreadPercent(), liquidity.observations(), derivatives.status(),
   96:                 derivatives.fundingRate(), derivatives.fundingPercentile(),
   97:                 derivatives.openInterestChangePercent(), derivatives.priceChangePercent(),
   98:                 derivatives.confidenceAdjustment(), sentimentEnabled,
   99:                 enabled, contributing, coverage, List.copyOf(evidence));
  100:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java 20-75 =====
   20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
   21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
   22:  */
   23: @Component
   24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
   25: @Slf4j
   26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
   27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
   28: 
   29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
   30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
   31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
   32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
   33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
   34:         }
   35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
   36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
   37:         BigDecimal minimum;
   38:         try {
   39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
   40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
   41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
   42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
   43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
   44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
   45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
   46:         }
   47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
   48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
   49:         }
   50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
   51:     }
   52: 
   53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
   54:     }
   55: }

  159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
  160:      *
  161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
  162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
  163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
  164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
  165:      */
  166:     @Transactional
  167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
  168:         if (indicator == null) {
  169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
  170:         }
  171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
  172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
  173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
  174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
  175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);

  208: 
  209:     private int rawConfidence(
  210:             MarketRegimeAssessment regime,
  211:             MarketContextSnapshot context,
  212:             MultiTimeframeConfluenceResult confluence,
  213:             BtcMarketContextResult btc,
  214:             DerivativesPositioningResult derivatives,
  215:             OrderBookLiquidityResult liquidity
  216:     ) {
  217:         double value = regime.confidence() * 0.25;
  218:         value += (context.dataValid() ? 100 : 0) * 0.15;
  219:         value += confluenceConfidence(confluence.status()) * 0.20;
  220:         value += btcConfidence(btc.contextStatus(), btc.stable()) * 0.15;
  221:         value += liquidityConfidence(liquidity.status(), liquidity.observations()) * 0.12;
  222:         value += derivativesConfidence(derivatives) * 0.08;
  223:         value += sentimentConfidence(context) * 0.10;
  224:         int result = (int) Math.round(value);
  225:         return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, result));
  226:     }
  227: 
  228:     private int confluenceConfidence(ConfluenceStatus status) {
  229:         return switch (status) {
  230:             case STRONG_AGREEMENT -> 100;
  231:             case AGREEMENT -> 90;
  232:             case MIXED -> 60;
  233:             case CONFLICT -> 30;
  234:             case STRONG_CONFLICT -> 10;
  235:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
  236:         };
  237:     }
  238: 
  239:     private int btcConfidence(BtcContextStatus status, boolean stable) {
  240:         int base = switch (status) {
  241:             case CONFIRMED -> 95;
  242:             case NOT_APPLICABLE -> 85;
  243:             case NEUTRAL -> 75;
  244:             case LEARNING -> 50;
  245:             case CONFLICT -> 30;
  246:             case STRONG_CONFLICT -> 10;
  247:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
  248:             case STALE_CONTEXT -> 0;
  249:         };
  250:         return stable ? base : Math.max(0, base - 10);
  251:     }
  252: 
  253:     private int liquidityConfidence(LiquidityContextStatus status, int observations) {
  254:         int base = switch (status) {
  255:             case BULLISH_SUPPORT -> 95;
  256:             case BALANCED, DISABLED -> 85;
  257:             case LEARNING -> 55;
  258:             case INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD -> 25;
  259:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
  260:             case BEARISH_PRESSURE, STOP_EXPOSED -> 35;
  261:             case TARGET_BLOCKED, THIN_LIQUIDITY -> 15;
  262:             case WALL_WEAKENING -> 65;
  263:         };
  264:         if (observations <= 0 && status != LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED) {
  265:             return Math.min(base, 40);
  266:         }
  267:         return base;
  268:     }
  269: 
  270:     private int derivativesConfidence(DerivativesPositioningResult result) {
  271:         int base = switch (result.status()) {
  272:             case FRESH_LONG_BUILDUP, FRESH_SHORT_BUILDUP -> 95;
  273:             case HEALTHY_BULLISH, HEALTHY_BEARISH, BALANCED, NOT_APPLICABLE -> 80;
  274:             case SHORT_COVERING, LONG_LIQUIDATION, LOW_CONVICTION -> 55;
  275:             case LONGS_CROWDED, SHORTS_CROWDED -> 25;
  276:             case LEARNING -> 50;
  277:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
  278:         };
  279:         return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, base + result.confidenceAdjustment()));
  280:     }
  281: 
  282:     private int sentimentConfidence(MarketContextSnapshot context) {
  283:         if (!context.sentimentEnabled()) {
  284:             return 70;
  285:         }
  286:         BigDecimal coverage = context.sentimentCoverage();
  287:         if (coverage == null) {
  288:             return 40;
  289:         }
  290:         BigDecimal percent = coverage.compareTo(BigDecimal.ONE) <= 0

  562:     private ExitDecision evaluateExit(long runId, ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s, TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
  563:         BigDecimal price = s.getLatestPrice();
  564:         if (price == null) return ExitDecision.hold();
  565: 
  566:         // Replay uses the exact same temporal price-authority rule as production. A signal
  567:         // generated later may still carry a candle close from before entry; that historical
  568:         // price may update MTF context but may not retroactively hit TP/SL/profit-lock.
  569:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime());
  570:         if (authoritativePrice && p.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(p.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
  571:             // Exact production TP-continuation call, including immutable entry structure.
  572:             // FIX-011 regression relies on Replay and live using this same shared policy.
  573:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
  574:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
  575:                     p.entryTrend(), p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(),
  576:                     p.entryConfidence(), p.entryScore());
  577:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
  578:                 BigDecimal distance = p.takeProfit().subtract(p.entryPrice());
  579:                 BigDecimal newTarget = p.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
  580:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", p.takeProfit(), newTarget, p, continuation.explanation());
  581:                 jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, p.positionId());
  582:                 return new ExitDecision(false, "EXTEND_TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), newTarget);
  583:             }
  584:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
  585:                     runId, s.getSymbol(), p.positionId(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
  586:                     p.entryTime(), p.entryPrice(), p.quantity(), p.cost(), p.stopLoss(), p.takeProfit(), p.highest(),
  587:                     p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.entryScore(), p.entryConfidence(), p.entryTrend(),
  588:                     p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(), oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
  589:                     continuation.explanation());
  590:             persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), p, continuation.explanation());
  591:             return new ExitDecision(true, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation());
  592:         }
  593:         // FIX-052 fallback ordering also mirrors Production: STOP_LOSS is absolute and
  594:         // is evaluated before profit-lock. Exact V64 runs normally take this path from
  595:         // persisted live-price events rather than from signal-carried prices.
  596:         if (authoritativePrice && p.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(p.stopLoss()) <= 0)
  597:             return new ExitDecision(true, "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the stored stop loss.");
  598: 
  599:         ShadowPosition updated = authoritativePrice ? profitLockState(p, price) : p;
  600:         if (authoritativePrice && (updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE
  601:                 || updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
  602:                 || p.profitLockActive() != updated.profitLockActive()
  603:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice())
  604:                 || p.profitLockState() != updated.profitLockState())) {
  605:             BigDecimal fix118Progress = updated.takeProfit() == null || updated.takeProfit().compareTo(updated.entryPrice()) <= 0
  606:                     ? BigDecimal.ZERO
  607:                     : updated.highest().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC)
  608:                             .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
  609:                             .divide(updated.takeProfit().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
  610:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_PATH] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, " +
  611:                             "entry={}, target={}, highest={}, previousActive={}, active={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousLock={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, triggered={}",
  612:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
  613:                     updated.entryPrice(), updated.takeProfit(), updated.highest(), p.profitLockActive(), updated.profitLockActive(),
  614:                     p.profitLockState(), updated.profitLockState(), p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice(), fix118Progress,
  615:                     updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
  616:                             && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0);
  617:         }
  618:         BigDecimal minimumProfitableExit = p.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005));
  619:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
  620:                 && price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
  621:             String floorReason = "TP-extension rebase safety floor was breached at " + minimumProfitableExit + ". "
  622:                     + profitLockConfigText();
  623:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, floor={}",
  624:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price, minimumProfitableExit);
  625:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
  626:             return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
  627:         }
  628:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
  629:                 && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
  630:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, lock={}, target={}",
  631:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
  632:                     updated.profitLockPrice(), updated.takeProfit());
  633:             if (price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
  634:                 String floorReason = "Profit-lock hard floor was breached; protected profit can no longer be preserved. "
  635:                         + profitLockConfigText();
  636:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
  637:                 return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
  638:             }
  639:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(
  640:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
  641:             if (!lockDecision.exit()) {
  642:                 String holdReason = lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
  643:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, holdReason);
  644:                 return ExitDecision.hold();
  645:             }
  646:             String lockReason = "Price retraced to the protected profit level after a profitable advance. "
  647:                     + lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
  648:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, lockReason);
  649:             return new ExitDecision(true, lockDecision.code(), lockReason);
  650:         }
  651:         // Production has two SELL authorities: the live HTF PositionExitPolicy and the
  652:         // 1m signal TradeExecutionValidationService. Replay executes both shared production
  653:         // policies in the same order instead of carrying a test-only SELL rule.
  654:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(
  655:                 oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
  656:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
  657:             return new ExitDecision(true, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation());
  658:         }
  659:         if ("1m".equals(s.getInterval()) && bearish(s.getDecision())) {
  660:             TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(s);

  168:     private PositionRecommendation hardRiskRecommendation(WalletManagedPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
  169:         if (p.getStopLossUsdt() != null && p.getStopLossUsdt().signum() > 0
  170:                 && price.compareTo(p.getStopLossUsdt()) <= 0) {
  171:             return PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS;
  172:         }
  173:         if (p.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null && p.getTakeProfitUsdt().signum() > 0
  174:                 && price.compareTo(p.getTakeProfitUsdt()) >= 0) {
  175:             return PositionRecommendation.TAKE_PROFIT;
  176:         }
  177:         return null;
  178:     }

  180:     private int profitProtection(BigDecimal pnlPercent, PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis) {
  181:         if (pnlPercent.signum() <= 0) return 0;
  182:         int score = 0;
  183:         if (pnlPercent.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(5)) >= 0) score += 2;
  184:         else if (pnlPercent.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(2)) >= 0) score += 1;
  185:         if (thesis.trendDrop() >= 6 || thesis.structureDrop() >= 3) score += 2;
  186:         if (thesis.momentumDrop() >= 5 || thesis.totalDrop() >= 15) score += 1;
  187:         return Math.min(5, score);
  188:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/EntryConsumptionPolicy.java 1-60 =====
    1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
    2: 
    3: import com.crypto.execution.domain.EntryConsumptionState;
    4: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletTradeRepository;
    5: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
    6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
    7: 
    8: /**
    9:  * FIX-112A: consumption belongs to the specific bullish signal, not to the
   10:  * symbol's general position state. A pre-existing position can coexist with a
   11:  * blocked progressive-add signal, so OPEN-position state is not proof that the
   12:  * immediately previous bullish signal actually executed.
   13:  */
   14: @Service
   15: @RequiredArgsConstructor
   16: public class EntryConsumptionPolicy {
   17:     private final WalletTradeRepository walletTradeRepository;
   18:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
   19: 
   20:     public EntryConsumptionState resolve(Long previousSignalId) {
   21:         if (previousSignalId == null) return EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
   22:         if (replayScope.active()) {
   23:             return replayScope.entryConsumed(previousSignalId)
   24:                     ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED
   25:                     : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
   26:         }
   27:         // Production authority is the immutable executed BUY row keyed to the
   28:         // exact triggering signal. This works for both first entries and adds.
   29:         boolean executed = walletTradeRepository
   30:                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
   31:                         previousSignalId, "BUY", "EXECUTED")
   32:                 .isPresent();
   33:         return executed ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
   34:     }
   35: }

  148:         boolean fiveMinuteStillBullish = isBullish(fiveMinute.getOriginalDecision());
  149:         if (fiveMinuteStillBullish) {
  150:             return Evaluation.hold(evidenceState, "HOLD_5M_BULLISH_SUPPORT",
  151:                     "Fresh 5m original BUY/STRONG_BUY support remains intact.", progress, giveback);
  152:         }
  153: 
  154:         if (bearishStreak < REQUIRED_CONSECUTIVE_BEARISH_1M) {
  155:             return Evaluation.hold(evidenceState, "HOLD_1M_BEARISH_NOT_PERSISTENT",
  156:                     "Fresh 1m underlying bearish evidence has not persisted for two distinct signals.",
  157:                     progress, giveback);
  158:         }
  159: 
  160:         State confirmed = new State(NearTpState.NEAR_TP_FAILURE_CONFIRMED,
  161:                 best, bearishStreak, lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId, false);
  162:         return Evaluation.harvest(confirmed, "NEAR_TP_FAILURE_CONFIRMED",
  163:                 "Near-TP failure confirmed: rejection remains active, 1m original bearish evidence persisted, "
  164:                         + "and fresh 5m original BUY/STRONG_BUY support is absent.", progress, giveback);
  165:     }
  166: 
  167:     public BigDecimal tpProgress(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal takeProfit, BigDecimal current) {
  168:         if (entry == null || takeProfit == null || current == null) return BigDecimal.ZERO;
  169:         BigDecimal plannedDistance = takeProfit.subtract(entry, MC);
  170:         if (plannedDistance.signum() <= 0) return BigDecimal.ZERO;
  171:         return current.subtract(entry, MC).divide(plannedDistance, MC)
  172:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC);
  173:     }
  174: 
  175:     private boolean isFreshAtOrBefore(TradeSignal signal, Instant evaluatedAt, Duration maxAge) {
  176:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || evaluatedAt == null) return false;
  177:         if (signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(evaluatedAt)) return false;
  178:         return Duration.between(signal.getGeneratedAt(), evaluatedAt).compareTo(maxAge) <= 0;
  179:     }
  180: 

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/BinanceRestAdmissionGate.java 1-100 =====
    1: package com.crypto.client.binance;
    2: 
    3: import java.time.Duration;
    4: import java.util.Locale;
    5: import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
    6: import java.util.function.LongSupplier;
    7: import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
    8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
    9: import org.springframework.web.client.RestClientResponseException;
   10: 
   11: /**
   12:  * Process-wide REST cooldown for Binance rate-limit responses. This is an
   13:  * admission check only: it never locks, queues, or fences trading/protection.
   14:  * Callers receive a retryable exception and keep their own bounded retry policy.
   15:  */
   16: @Component
   17: public class BinanceRestAdmissionGate {
   18:     private static final long DEFAULT_429_MS = 60_000L;
   19:     private static final long DEFAULT_418_MS = 15 * 60_000L;
   20:     private final AtomicLong blockedUntilNanos = new AtomicLong();
   21:     private final LongSupplier nanoTime;
   22: 
   23:     public BinanceRestAdmissionGate() { this(System::nanoTime); }
   24:     BinanceRestAdmissionGate(LongSupplier nanoTime) { this.nanoTime = nanoTime; }
   25: 
   26:     public void admit() {
   27:         long remaining = blockedUntilNanos.get() - nanoTime.getAsLong();
   28:         if (remaining > 0) throw new BinanceRestAdmissionDeferredException(Duration.ofNanos(remaining));
   29:     }
   30: 
   31:     public void recordFailure(RuntimeException failure) {
   32:         if (!(failure instanceof RestClientResponseException response)) return;
   33:         int status = response.getStatusCode().value();
   34:         if (status != 429 && status != 418) return;
   35:         long millis = retryAfterMillis(response.getResponseHeaders(), status == 418 ? DEFAULT_418_MS : DEFAULT_429_MS);
   36:         blockFor(Duration.ofMillis(millis));
   37:     }
   38: 
   39:     void blockFor(Duration delay) {
   40:         long nanos = Math.max(0L, delay.toNanos());
   41:         long candidate = saturatingAdd(nanoTime.getAsLong(), nanos);
   42:         blockedUntilNanos.accumulateAndGet(candidate, Math::max);
   43:     }
   44: 
   45:     private static long retryAfterMillis(HttpHeaders headers, long fallback) {
   46:         if (headers == null) return fallback;
   47:         String raw = headers.getFirst(HttpHeaders.RETRY_AFTER);
   48:         if (raw == null || raw.isBlank()) return fallback;
   49:         try { return Math.max(1_000L, Long.parseLong(raw.trim()) * 1_000L); }
   50:         catch (NumberFormatException ignored) { return fallback; }
   51:     }
   52:     private static long saturatingAdd(long a, long b) { return b > 0 && a > Long.MAX_VALUE - b ? Long.MAX_VALUE : a + b; }
   53: 
   54:     public static final class BinanceRestAdmissionDeferredException extends RuntimeException {
   55:         private final Duration retryAfter;
   56:         BinanceRestAdmissionDeferredException(Duration retryAfter) {
   57:             super("Binance REST admission is paused for " + retryAfter.toMillis() + " ms");
   58:             this.retryAfter = retryAfter;
   59:         }
   60:         public Duration retryAfter() { return retryAfter; }
   61:     }
   62: }

===== agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md 1-45 =====
    1: # Replay / Production Business-Logic-Parity Redesign
    2: 
    3: **Revision:** RPR-0.4-DRAFT  
    4: **Status:** DRAFT ƒ?" NOT APPROVED ƒ?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
    5: **Created:** 2026-10-10  
    6: **Scope owner:** Codex implementation / Claude independent review
    7: 
    8: ## 1. Business objective
    9: 
   10: Make manual Replay reproduce the current paper-trading Production business flow
   11: as closely as durable historical evidence permits, while remaining completely
   12: isolated from Production trading state and wallet data.
   13: 
   14: The exact intended flow is:
   15: 
   16: ```text
   17: historical candle + historical context + canonical accepted price events
   18:   -> shared Production indicator/signal/decision policies
   19:   -> replay-local virtual wallet/account state
   20:   -> shared sizing/allocation/entry revalidation policies
   21:   -> replay-local position lifecycle
   22:   -> shared TP/SL/profit-lock/continuation/exit policies
   23:   -> replay output + explicit parity certificate
   24: ```
   25: 
   26: Replay must never write `wallet_*`, `paper_position`, `trade_signal`,
   27: `signal_processing_work`, or any other Production trading table.
   28: 
   29: The result is not a claim that historical Java scheduling, MySQL lock timing,
   30: network delay, external Binance availability, or the historical Production
   31: wallet balance can be reproduced. It is a verified **business-logic parity**
   32: claim only when all required market/decision evidence exists. Otherwise Replay
   33: remains available as a labelled simulation and may not claim parity.
   34: 
   35: ## 2. Confirmed root cause and evidence
   36: 
   37: The 2026-10-10 source audit established the following:
   38: 
   39: 1. Signal, entry, normal-exit, continuation, profit-lock, and near-TP policy
   40:    use several shared Production services.
   41: 2. `ShadowProductionReplayService` separately owns replay wallet cash,
   42:    positions, daily budget, persistence, price-loop lifecycle, and result
   43:    labels. It starts at `INITIAL_CAPITAL=10000`, runs one symbol/position, and
   44:    does not reproduce Production wallet/global controls.
   45: 3. A run is labelled `EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY` when at least one accepted price

  464:     /**
  465:      * FIX-11H: loads one immutable replay dataset. The prefix comes from the exact existing
  466:      * closed/as-of query, so missing candle gaps cannot shorten history. The forward segment
  467:      * explicitly requires closed=true. No Production caller reaches this method.
  468:      */
  469:     private ReplayDataset loadDataset(String symbol, String btcSymbol, Instant start, Instant end) {
  470:         Map<ReplayDataset.CandleKey, List<Candle>> candleMap = new HashMap<>();
  471:         Set<String> symbols = new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of(symbol, btcSymbol));
  472:         int historyLimit = technicalIndicatorService.regressionHistoryLimit();
  473:         for (String sym : symbols) {
  474:             for (String interval : List.of("1m", "5m", "1h")) {
  475:                 List<Candle> prefix = new java.util.ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
  476:                         sym, interval, start, PageRequest.of(0, historyLimit)));
  477:                 java.util.Collections.reverse(prefix);
  478:                 List<Candle> forward = candleRepository
  479:                         .findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
  480:                                 sym, interval, start, end);
  481:                 List<Candle> merged = new java.util.ArrayList<>(prefix.size() + forward.size());
  482:                 merged.addAll(prefix);
  483:                 Instant prefixEnd = prefix.isEmpty() ? Instant.EPOCH : prefix.get(prefix.size() - 1).getOpenTime();
  484:                 forward.stream().filter(c -> c.getOpenTime().isAfter(prefixEnd)).forEach(merged::add);
  485:                 candleMap.put(new ReplayDataset.CandleKey(sym, interval), merged);
  486:                 log.info("FIX-11H dataset candles: symbol={}, interval={}, prefix={}, forward={}, merged={}",
  487:                         sym, interval, prefix.size(), forward.size(), merged.size());
  488:             }
  489:         }
  490: 
  491:         Map<ReplayDataset.SignalKey, Long> lineage = new HashMap<>();
  492:         // FIX-11H / FIX-112D: lineage is candle identity, not signal persistence timing.
  493:         // Recovery/backfilled Production signals can be generated hours after their candle, so
  494:         // generatedAt MUST NOT define this preload window. Select and match lineage by the same
  495:         // exact candleOpenTime identity used by ReplayDataset.sourceSignalId().
  496:         for (TradeSignal signal : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndCandleOpenTimeBetweenOrderByCandleOpenTimeAsc(symbol, start, end)) {
  497:             ReplayDataset.SignalKey key = new ReplayDataset.SignalKey(
  498:                     signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), signal.getCandleOpenTime());
  499:             Long previous = lineage.putIfAbsent(key, signal.getId());
  500:             if (previous != null && !previous.equals(signal.getId())) {
  501:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Replay lineage invariant violated for " + key
  502:                         + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
  503:             }
  504:         }
  505:         log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
  506:         return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);
  507:     }

   38:     public void record(TradeSignal reference, String path,
   39:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
   40:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
   41:         // Replay signals are JDBC-generated and have no entity id. Replay parity
   42:         // uses its own evidence tables; never write a misleading Production audit.
   43:         if (reference == null || assessment == null || reference.getId() == null
   44:                 || (replayScope != null && replayScope.active())) return;
   45:         AuditPayload payload = AuditPayload.from(reference, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
   46:         try {
   47:             executor.execute(() -> persist(payload));
   48:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException rejected) {
   49:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
   50:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit queue is full; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
   51:                     path, reference.getId());
   52:         }
   53:     }

   62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
   63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
   64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
   65: 
   66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
   67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
   68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
   69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
   70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
   71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
   72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
   73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
   74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
   75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
   76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
   77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
   78:             }
   79:         }
   80: 
   81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
   82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
   83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
   84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
   85:         }
   86: 
   87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
   88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
   89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
   90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
   91:         }
   92: 
   93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
   94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
   95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
   96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
   97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
   98:         }
   99: 
  100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
  101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
  102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
  103:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
  104:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
  105:             // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
  106:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
  107:                     executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
  108:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
  109:         };
  110:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
  111:         audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
  112:         return result;
  113:     }

  115:     /**
  116:      * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
  117:      * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
  118:      * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
  119:      * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
  120:      * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the
  121:      * normal BUY path would accept.
  122:      */
  123:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
  124:         return validateBuyContext(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE");
  125:     }

  127:     /** The caller path is audit evidence only; context eligibility remains identical. */
  128:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String auditPath) {
  129:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
  130:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution reference signal is missing required timing data.");
  131:         }
  132: 
  133:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  134:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  135:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  136:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
  137:         }
  138: 
  139:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  140:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  141:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  142:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
  143:         }
  144: 
  145:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  146:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  147:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  148:             audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
  149:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
  150:         }
  151: 
  152:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings().getExecutionProfile());
  153:         TradeSignal oneMinuteQuality = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
  154:         TradeSignal qualitySignal = oneMinuteQuality == null ? referenceSignal : oneMinuteQuality;
  155:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
  156:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
  157:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
  158:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(qualitySignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
  159:         };
  160:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
  161:         audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
  162:         return result;
  163:     }

  153:     /**
  154:      * FIX-11T: read the symbol's current Binance minimum executable notional directly
  155:      * from exchangeInfo.  Near-TP uses this only as a minimum-order guard; it does not
  156:      * introduce a separate quantity-rounding model. NOTIONAL is preferred when present,
  157:      * with MIN_NOTIONAL retained for symbols that still expose the older filter.
  158:      */
  159:     public BigDecimal getMinimumExecutableNotional(String symbol) {
  160:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
  161:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
  162:         }
  163: 
  164:         Map<String, Object> response = request(() -> restClient.get()
  165:             .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder
  166:                 .path("/api/v3/exchangeInfo")
  167:                 .queryParam("symbol", symbol.toUpperCase())
  168:                 .build())
  169:             .retrieve()
  170:             .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<>() {
  171:             }));
  172: 
  173:         if (response == null) {
  174:             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty Binance exchangeInfo response for " + symbol);
  175:         }
  176:         Object symbolsValue = response.get("symbols");
  177:         if (!(symbolsValue instanceof List<?> symbols) || symbols.isEmpty() || !(symbols.get(0) instanceof Map<?, ?> symbolInfo)) {
  178:             throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not contain symbol rules for " + symbol);
  179:         }
  180:         Object filtersValue = symbolInfo.get("filters");
  181:         if (!(filtersValue instanceof List<?> filters)) {
  182:             throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not contain filters for " + symbol);
  183:         }
  184: 
  185:         BigDecimal minNotional = null;
  186:         for (Object filterValue : filters) {
  187:             if (!(filterValue instanceof Map<?, ?> filter)) continue;
  188:             String filterType = String.valueOf(filter.get("filterType"));
  189:             if (!"NOTIONAL".equals(filterType) && !"MIN_NOTIONAL".equals(filterType)) continue;
  190:             Object raw = filter.get("minNotional");
  191:             if (raw == null) continue;
  192:             BigDecimal candidate = new BigDecimal(String.valueOf(raw));
  193:             if (candidate.signum() <= 0) continue;
  194:             if ("NOTIONAL".equals(filterType)) return candidate;
  195:             minNotional = candidate;
  196:         }
  197:         if (minNotional != null) return minNotional;
  198:         throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not expose a minimum notional for " + symbol);
  199:     }
  200:

  202:         if (row == null || row.size() < 11) {
  203:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
  204:                 "Invalid Binance kline response"
  205:             );
  206:         }
  207: 
  208:         return new BinanceKline(
  209:             Instant.ofEpochMilli(toLong(row.get(0))),
  210:             toDecimal(row.get(1)),

## FINDINGS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** RPR-0.6 is not a valid frozen implementation contract: governance requires the approved exact revision at `agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md`, while RPR-0.6 is a separate draft and defers the dirty-worktree freeze. Current `HEAD` matches its declared commit, but the worktree is materially dirty. [AGENTS.md](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:327) · [canonical contract](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:3) · [RPR-0.6](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md:5)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The proposed read/write boundary is incomplete and contains a nonexistent table name (`regression_test_run`; actual table is `analysis_test_run`). Current Replay writes many test, archive, revision, and diagnostic tables; `fix122_evaluation` is written in an independent transaction, so “discard staged artifacts” cannot be implemented safely without an explicit compensating/publication design. Current chart output also reads forbidden Production `wallet_trade`. [spec boundary](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md:52) · [schema](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql:1046) · [Replay writes/purges](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestService.java:350) · [independent audit write](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceStore.java:32) · [Production wallet read](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestService.java:549)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Account and HTTP isolation lack a fail-closed design. Replay currently reads `wallet_settings`; shared execution validation also reads it. The current minimum-notional policy makes an HTTP `exchangeInfo` call. The proposed profile port says what must not happen, but not which boundary enforces it across the async worker and shared services. The present scope is a `ThreadLocal`, therefore it does not automatically cover work moved to another thread. [wallet read](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:999) · [shared settings read](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:400) · [HTTP policy](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java:29) · [thread-local scope](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:31)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** `ANALYSIS_PARITY` is not defined precisely enough to validate. The existing comparator merely compares selected fields; RPR-0.6 omits the eligibility set, match key, field-set version, tolerances, and publication/refusal decision. Further, historical Replay deliberately produces derivatives `UNAVAILABLE` when enabled, while Production live analysis calls the live evaluator and persists derivatives outcomes on `trade_signal`; raw confidence gives derivatives an 8% weight. RPR’s vague “stored as-of evidence, recomputation, or refusal” does not state which route applies or register the needed analysis/context services. [validator](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java:17) · [historical derivatives](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/DerivativesPositioningService.java:38) · [persisted derivatives fields](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/TradeSignal.java:120) · [confidence calculation](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/FinalDecisionService.java:209)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The claimed shared-policy simulation is not yet reproducible. Production’s signal path has a specific ordered sequence—position-management advisory/possible stop, TP, profit lock, stop, hard floor, validated SELL, then progressive add—while Replay’s exact-price branch separately processes prices and only adds a bearish `validateSell` step at signal time. RPR M06 does not define independent price-path and signal-path sequences, execution/decision clocks, or collision handling. [Production order](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:113) · [Replay ordering](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:122) · [RPR M06](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md:91)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Current Replay signal IDs are null, disabling ID-dependent near-TP evidence and entry-consumption behavior. RPR correctly proposes stable non-Production IDs, but does not specify a collision-proof namespace, every null-ID shortcut, or the required ordering: no-HTTP virtual constraints must be in place before those IDs make previously skipped paths reachable. [ID omitted](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:741) · [consumption behavior](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/EntryConsumptionPolicy.java:20) · [near-TP ID dependency](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.java:119)

## EVIDENCE

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The API has no virtual-profile or parity-mode input, and inserts the run and FIX-122 revision before the async worker begins. The investigation-case path hard-codes `DATABASE`; this path is missing from RPR’s registry. - **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** `DATABASE` Replay loads candles without a closed-candle filter, unlike `DATASET`; RPR must state whether both modes remain, are replaced, or are separately qualified. - **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The source baseline needs immutable source/config manifesting before approval, not solely before implementation. The reviewed RPR-0.6 hash is `5C057C181459727E7D62F580F525FDB4392E15BBEAD7A27F5BAA2F3AAADF5A03`; no production, database, runtime, or secrets were accessed.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Any shared-class implementation has an operational rollback requirement: the checked-in Jenkins pipeline builds and then deploys LIVE without a separate approval stage. ## OBJECTIONS

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Reject approval until a revised, canonical, frozen specification provides a complete dependency/read/write/state inventory, including archive/proven paths, asynchronous state, and all prohibited Production dependencies.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Define the isolation enforcement mechanism, virtual-profile schema and dated constraints; prove fail-closed behavior for account reads/writes and HTTP across every thread and transaction.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Define an `ANALYSIS_PARITY` certificate: eligibility, identity key, versioned fields/tolerances, context availability-time rules, derivatives/order-book/sentiment treatment, and exact refusal codes.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Specify decision and fill clocks; separate price-event and signal-event orders; define same-boundary higher-timeframe visibility, daily-limit behavior, global-limit semantics for the virtual account, staging/publication/recovery, and surviving independent writes.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Register every affected controller/request/service/policy/config/migration/test/pipeline artifact, including explicit “unchanged because” entries where appropriate. Add measurable resource/latency limits and a rollback plan for shared Production classes.

## UNKNOWNS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** I did not establish whether derivatives were enabled in any environment, inspect Flyway history, run tests, or access a database, logs, or production systems.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** I do not accept as fact the claim that closed Binance messages necessarily arrive after nominal candle close; current source defines Replay’s intended ordering, but proving real event timing requires bounded stored-event measurement.
